# A.3 Verbatim Propositions and Thematic Synthesis {#sec-appendix-a-verbatim-ledger}

::: {.remark #rem-appendix-a-epistemic-boundary title="Epistemic Boundary Declaration for the Verbatim Ledger and Appendix A"}
**Literature result, not formalized in this book (for external literature results summarized in the synthesis column below; see the full three-tier epistemic boundary note at the bottom of this box).** The table below catalogs the **foundational authorial propositions** spanning the Prelude, Chapters 1–39, the Coda, and [Appendix D](appendix-d.md)–[Appendix E](appendix-e.md), mapping each original insight to its exact geometric mechanism and chapter anchor:

| **A02** (`PDF-02`) | *A quantum state is a direction, and a quantum gate turns that direction, so it can be understood as a geometric structure with area.* | 1D state ray vs. 2D bivector rotation area element $\mathbf{u}\wedge\mathbf{v}$ | Ch 5 (Epigraph) |
